# Wave digest: 12 slices, 177 findings

### ARCH-09 [high] The restore-into-a-new-project runbook never repoints the static site: the Pages build variables, the rebuild, the Resend webhook endpoint and the gate still name the old project
docs/operations.md:1374 fix:trivial conf:high
EVIDENCE: docs/operations.md:1332-1407 (steps 1-9) restores the dumps, Vault, cron jobs, function secrets (noting 'A new project has a new <project-ref>, so FUNCTIONS_PUBLIC_URL and the webhook address in Moyasar's dashboard change with it'), Auth settings and objects, then a smoke test 'through the contact form from the site'. Nothing changes the Pages build's NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL and NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY or rebuilds: Next inlines them into every bundle and page (src/lib/media-ref.ts:123-125 builds every library image URL from it; ContactForm.tsx:107, quote.ts:318, VariantAction.tsx:57 and the admin client call it). The Resend endpoint registered at operations.md:338-340 is `https://<project-ref>.supabase.co/functions/v1/resend-webhook`, and SITE-STATE's gate reads `<SUPABASE_URL>/rest/v1/site_state` (SITE-STATE-CONTRACT.md:43); a configured Google client's redirect URI is the old project's Auth callback too.
IMPACT: Two outcomes, depending on the old project. If it is gone, nothing on the site reaches the restored project: images vanish and the contact form, cart, checkout and staff sign-in fail, and the runbook has no step that explains why. If it still answers (a restore after a bad migration or an accidental deletion, which is what a restore into a new project is for), the step-5 smoke test passes against the OLD project, so the operator believes the restore is live: the site and the admin (built with the same URL) keep creating and showing orders and messages in the old database, while the Moyasar webhook, repointed to the new project as step 5 says, delivers to a project that does not know those invoices (review payments and owner alerts there). Every order and message written after the restore exists only in the old project; repointing later or deleting the old project loses them.
FIX: Add a step between 5 and the smoke test: update the Pages project's NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL and NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY (production and preview), set the new `pages_deploy_hook` Vault value if the Pages project changed, trigger a build and wait for it; repoint the Resend webhook endpoint (and re-set RESEND_WEBHOOK_SECRET if Resend issues a new one), the Google OAuth redirect URI when Google sign-in is on, and the gate's two Supabase variables.

### SUPPLY-11 [high] The restore rehearsal loads the whole production database into a stack whose ports Docker publishes on every interface, behind the CLI's public default credentials
scripts/restore-check.mjs:423 RUNTIME fix:small conf:medium
EVIDENCE: restore-check.mjs:423 runs `supabase start --workdir <scratch>` on the `supabase init` defaults (patchConfig only moves ports +1000 and turns services off), then loads data.sql (auth.users, auth.mfa_factors, every finance and customer row) and uploads every paid file. Supabase CLI v2.106.0 publishes ports with no host IP (apps/cli-go/internal/db/start/start.go:121 `nat.PortBinding{{HostPort: hostPort}}`; internal/start/start.go:556-557 for Kong) and defaults to `Password: "postgres"` (pkg/config/config.go:366) and public API secrets (pkg/config/apikeys.go:17-20, defaultJwtSecret and sb_secret_N7UND…). `docker ps` on this machine shows the running CLI stack as `0.0.0.0:54322->5432/tcp, [::]:54322->5432/tcp`. Docker: ports mapped without a host address are published `to all host addresses (0.0.0.0 and [::])` (https://docs.docker.com/engine/network/port-publishing/); the daemon `ip` default does not apply to user-defined networks such as the CLI's (https://docs.docker.com/engine/network/drivers/bridge/).
IMPACT: For the minutes a rehearsal runs, any device that can reach the owner's machine (home or office Wi-Fi, a café; on Windows when Docker Desktop's firewall prompt was allowed, on macOS by default) can run `psql postgresql://postgres:postgres@<owner-ip>:55322/postgres` and copy every customer, order and address and the owner's TOTP secret, or pull the paid files through the API on 55321 with the stack's default service key. D35 encrypts backups so that this plaintext never leaves the owner's control.
FIX: Before `supabase start`, create the scratch network bound to loopback: `docker network create -o com.docker.network.bridge.host_binding_ipv4=127.0.0.1 supabase_network_anasaq-restore-check` (the CLI reuses an existing network: internal/utils/docker.go:68-71); after start, read `docker port supabase_db_anasaq-restore-check` and refuse to restore unless every binding is 127.0.0.1.
PROOF: Run `pnpm restore-check <file>` on a disposable backup; while it prints `Uploading storage objects...`, run `docker port supabase_db_anasaq-restore-check` (wrong result: `5432/tcp -> 0.0.0.0:55322` and `[::]:55322`), then from a second device on the same network `psql postgresql://postgres:postgres@<machine LAN IP>:55322/postgres -c "select count(*) from auth.users"` (wrong result: the restored row count instead of a refused connection).

### VENDOR-PLAT-04 [high] The 18 room films are served from the Pages origin, which answers Range requests with 200 and the whole file; Safari on iPhone and Mac needs 206 to play MP4, so the films are likely to fail on Apple devices
src/components/weave/VideoTile.tsx:97 RUNTIME fix:small conf:medium prior:I33 (where the films live) is open but does not consider range support; I32's file-reply Range check covers only the paid file on Storage
EVIDENCE: VideoTile.tsx:97 `<source src={`/${entry.file}`} type="video/mp4" />`: every film (/started, /built, /passed; public/media/*.mp4, up to 11.4 MB, I33) is a same-origin static file, so at launch Cloudflare Pages serves it. Cloudflare Pages (https://developers.cloudflare.com/pages/configuration/serving-pages/, updated Apr 21, 2026): 'Pages currently returns 200 responses for HTTP range requests; however, the team is working on adding spec-compliant 206 partial responses.' Live check 2026-10-07 on the owner's own Pages project: `curl -H 'Range: bytes=100-199' https://anas-v2-ansaq.pages.dev/uploads/anas-portrait.jpg` -> `HTTP/1.1 200 OK`, `Content-Length: 318857`, no Content-Range or Accept-Ranges. Apple (Safari Web Content Guide, 'Configuring Your Server'): 'HTTP servers hosting media files for iOS must support byte-range requests, which iOS uses to perform random access in media playback.' Cloudflare (https://developers.cloudflare.com/cache/troubleshooting/mp4-videos-on-ios-and-safari/, May 4, 2026): 'Safari on macOS and iOS devices may fail to load MP4 video files ... Safari and iOS devices rely on HTTP range requests'; its fix forwards ranges to an origin that honours them, which Pages does not. ISSUES.md I33 offers committing the films to Pages ('each is under Pages' 25 MiB file limit') as one of two options; local runs serve ranges and Playwright's WebKit does not use Apple's media stack, so no suite would notice.
IMPACT: An iPhone or Mac Safari visitor (a large share of Saudi visitors) taps «تشغيل» on any of Anas's films and gets «تعذّر تشغيل المقطع. حاول مرة أخرى.» or a black frame, on three of the eight rooms, from launch day. Seeking and resuming also break where playback starts at all.
FIX: Decide I33 for a host that answers Range with 206 before P10's hosted preview: a public R2 bucket on a media subdomain (range support, no egress fee) or the Supabase Storage public bucket (its renderer sets Accept-Ranges and Content-Range, but each play counts against the 5 GB Free egress), and point public/media/manifest.json `file` at it. Add to I32/P10: on the preview, `curl -H 'Range: bytes=0-1'` a film URL must answer 206, and one film must play on a real iPhone.
PROOF: Deploy the export to a Pages preview with the films committed. `curl -sI -H 'Range: bytes=0-1' https://<preview>.pages.dev/media/raha-drone-opening_landscape.mp4`: wrong result is 200 with the full Content-Length and no Content-Range. Then open /built in Safari on an iPhone and tap «تشغيل» on the drone film: wrong result is the failure status or a frame that never starts.

### ARCH-01 [medium] A Pages build that fails after the deploy hook answered raises no signal anywhere while the owner home says «بناء الموقع: سليم»; Cloudflare's free 'Deployment failed' notification is in no plan step, and I34 records the failure as dashboard-only
PLANS/ISSUES.md:25 fix:trivial conf:high prior:I34 (known open, record wrong about 'visible only in the Pages dashboard')
EVIDENCE: site_build_trigger records a run 'ok' when the hook answers 2xx (20260930140000_audit2_fixes.sql:737 `v_ok := v_answered and v_status between 200 and 299 ...`), and AdminHome renders it as «سليم» (src/components/admin/AdminHome.tsx:31). I34 (ISSUES.md:25) says 'a Pages build that breaks after the hook answered is visible only in the Pages dashboard; the owner checks it after the first publishes', and docs/operations.md:397-399 repeats it. Cloudflare's Notifications list 'Pages > Project updates' with the event 'Deployment failed', filterable by project and environment, 'Included with: All Cloudflare plans' (https://developers.cloudflare.com/notifications/notification-available/, last updated 2026-10-02). grep of PLANS/ and docs/ finds no mention of it.
IMPACT: Any later build failure (an invalid published document, a transient Supabase error or timeout during the build's fetches, a broken commit on main, the build quota) leaves the last good deploy live with no alert: a post archived for a legal reason, a corrected price or a product taken off sale stay on the public site while the owner home says the build is healthy; after a policy change and its re-approval the checkout page keeps the old revisions, so every buyer sees «نحدّث السياسات الآن؛ حاول بعد قليل.» with the button disabled (CheckoutForm.tsx:205, 782-790; src/lib/cart.ts:432-448) until a build succeeds, while the owner's screen says «الشراء مفتوح». The record frames it as a first-publishes check, but it can happen on any publish.
FIX: Add to P11's hosted setup (WORK-PACKAGES P11, docs/deployment.md) and to I34: create a Cloudflare notification 'Pages: Project updates' for the production environment of the Pages project with the event 'Deployment failed', delivered to the owner's email; correct I34 and docs/operations.md:397-399 to say the failure is mailed by Cloudflare and the owner home only knows the hook accepted the request (relabel the ok run, e.g. «طُلب البناء», so «سليم» is not read as 'the site is updated').

### ARCH-02 [medium] ARCHITECTURE says a paused Free project is 'harmless before orders', but the launched static site needs Supabase at view time: every library image, the contact form, live availability, the cart and the notify sign-up, and every rebuild
PLANS/ARCHITECTURE.md:55 OWNER fix:trivial conf:high
EVIDENCE: ARCHITECTURE.md:55: 'The hosted Free project may pause while idle; that is harmless before orders.' DECISIONS.md:87 leaves 'whether the site launches on Free before orders' to the owner (D34 launches before payments). But src/lib/media-ref.ts:123-125 bakes `${NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL}/storage/v1/object/public/media-public` into every library image (Picture.tsx renders it as <img src>), ContactForm.tsx:107, AvailabilityForm.tsx:41, VariantAction.tsx:57 and quote.ts:318/398 call the project from the browser, and every Pages build reads it (content.ts, store.ts, journal.ts, policies.ts). Supabase: a Free project with too little 'user database activity' over 7 days is paused and must be resumed from the dashboard (https://supabase.com/docs/guides/platform/free-project-pausing); a Free organization that continually exceeds its quota (5 GB egress + 5 GB cached, https://supabase.com/docs/guides/platform/manage-your-usage/egress) can be restricted by 'Pausing projects' or 'Responding with a 402 status code for all API requests' (https://supabase.com/docs/guides/platform/billing-faq#fair-use-policy). Library images are Storage egress.
IMPACT: If the owner launches on Free, as the plan allows, a quiet week or an image-heavy traffic month takes down every image Anas uploaded through the admin (journal covers, product covers, new scenes and room pictures), the contact form, the store's live state and the cart, and stops every rebuild (the build fails and the old deploy stays), until someone resumes the project or the billing cycle resets. The plan tells him this is harmless, so the choice is made on wrong information.
FIX: Replace the sentence in ARCHITECTURE.md:55 and DECISIONS.md:87 with what a pause or a Fair Use restriction breaks on the launched site (the list above) and that resuming is a manual dashboard step; add to P11's launch checklist the choice 'Pro at launch, or Free with the owner watching Supabase's pause and quota mails', and list the Storage egress of library images in docs/costs.md.

### ARCH-03 [medium] DATA-AND-SECURITY promises order lines that are 'variant or service exactly one', but finance.order_items requires a product variant, and every P08 money function keys on variant lines; P09's package lists none of them
supabase/migrations/20260927160000_catalog_and_checkout.sql:389 fix:small conf:high prior:wave A DB-PAY plan gap 'P09 paid bookings must settle through the P08 payment core'; the order_items sentence dates from P07 and is not among P08-CONTRACT section 13's recorded deviations, so the planned P08-close doc update may not reach it
EVIDENCE: PLANS/DATA-AND-SECURITY.md:35: 'orders / order_items | ... Item variant or service exactly one'. The table: `variant_id uuid not null references public.product_variants (id), product_id uuid not null ..., sku text not null, ... fulfillment text not null check (fulfillment in ('digital', 'physical', 'signed'))` and `unique (order_id, variant_id)` (catalog_and_checkout.sql:389-402). finance.order_try_commit (payment_core.sql:617-726) locks `public.product_variants` for every line, joins `v.id = i.variant_id`, checks stock or preorder capacity, decrements stock, and creates entitlements only for fulfillment 'digital' and fulfilments only for 'physical'/'signed'; payment_attempts.order_id is NOT NULL (payment_core.sql:140), so a paid booking can only settle as an order. WORK-PACKAGES P09 Files list `_shared/{booking,calendar,checkout,email}.ts`, the bookings and calendar functions and 'finance migrations', but not payments.ts, refunds.ts, orders.ts, OrderPage, the admin order and refund views, or the commit, expiry, refund and resolution functions that would have to learn a service line.
IMPACT: P09 starts from a contract that says the line model already holds a service; the first booking round finds it cannot insert a booking line without altering a NOT NULL money table on a live database and teaching order_try_commit, apply_verified_payment's late-payment path, order_resolve, checkout_expire, the refund allocation, the receipt and the order page about it. Done inside P09's allowlist it cannot be done; done as a parallel 'booking order' it settles every paid booking as an UNMAPPED_INVOICE review (wave A DB-PAY plan gap).
FIX: Correct DATA-AND-SECURITY:35 to what is built, and before P09 dispatch decide the booking line shape (a nullable variant_id plus service_id/booking_id with a one-of check and a 'booking' fulfillment kind, or a booking row linked to the order) and add to P09's file list the P08 paths that must branch on it: payment_core's order_try_commit and apply_verified_payment, order_resolve, checkout_expire, the refund functions and refunds.ts, orders.ts/OrderPage.tsx, the admin order views and the receipt mail.

### ARCH-13 [medium] Eleven of the fifteen pg_cron jobs are pure SQL, record no run and can fail every minute or every night unseen: nothing reads cron.job_run_details, and the owner home watches four jobs
src/components/admin/AdminHome.tsx:33 fix:small conf:high prior:wave A DB-CORE-12 (info: the contacts purge alone leaves no trace); wave A CONTRACT-02 / P08-CKPT-03 (payments_reconcile has no health line)
EVIDENCE: AdminHome's KNOWN_JOBS = ['email_outbox', 'site_build', 'media_sweep', 'backup'] (AdminHome.tsx:33). finance.job_runs is written only by public.job_run_record, which the Edge Functions call (20260926120000_contacts_and_email.sql:447-454), and by site_build_trigger (grep of `insert into finance.job_runs`); content-publish-due, checkout-expire, availability-sweep, notify-confirm-backlog, buyer-retention, contacts-purge, notifications-purge, payment-events-purge, rate-limits-purge, job-runs-purge and cron-run-details-purge run SQL directly (scripts/lib/cron-jobs.mjs listing). A failing run leaves its error only in cron.job_run_details, which no function, screen or script reads and which cron-run-details-purge deletes after 7 days (20260930140000_audit2_fixes.sql:820-823). buyer_retention_purge deletes through finance.orders_delete (20261002170000_stats_disputes.sql:78-95), which knows payment_events, payment_attempts, order_items, inventory_reservations and coupon_redemptions; any new table referencing finance.orders (P09's bookings, DATA-AND-SECURITY:41 'order optional') makes the delete raise and the whole purge roll back.
IMPACT: A job that starts failing after a migration (the likely case: P09 adds a foreign key to orders and the nightly buyer-retention purge fails every night) stops silently: D42's 90-day deletion of unpaid buyers' data, which the privacy policy is to state, simply stops; a failing checkout-expire leaves holds open, a failing availability-sweep stops restock mail, a failing publish_due stops scheduled posts. The owner home stays green and the evidence is purged after a week.
FIX: Add one owner/operations SQL function over cron.job_run_details (failed runs in the last 24 hours, by jobname, count and last time; no command text) and show it on the owner home as one line («مهمة مجدولة تفشل: …»); and add 'extend finance.orders_delete for every new child of finance.orders' to P09's file list.

### DOCS-02 [medium] EXECUTION-STATUS 'Current state' Lock row says the lock is taken again for round 12a; 12a and 12b are done
PLANS/EXECUTION-STATUS.md:12 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:DOCS-03; AUDIT-1 S22.1 (the same table fixed 2026-09-30, stale again)
EVIDENCE: EXECUTION-STATUS.md:12: "| Lock | None: P08 released `.anasaq-execution.lock` on 2026-10-03 after round 11c, for the pause. It is taken again for round 12a (`PLANS/HANDOFF.md`, \"How to continue P08\"). |". git log: 75a845f is round 12a, 3a30a91 is round 12b; the 12b commit's diff of EXECUTION-STATUS.md touches only the ledger and Next work (hunks at 358+), not this table. HANDOFF.md:8: "P08 paused after round 12b ... The next session takes the lock again and starts with the auditor's checkpoint (c)." KICKOFF.md step 1 lists this table, not HANDOFF, as what a new session reads first.

FIX: Rewrite the Lock row: 'None after 3a30a91 (round 12b, 2026-10-03); the next P08 session takes it for checkpoint (c), the battery and the plan documents.' While FABLE-AUDIT holds the lock, say so.

### FIX-A1-04 [medium] AUDIT-1 S22.1 regressed: EXECUTION-STATUS's Current state table says P08 waits for round 12a and its lock, two rounds after both were committed
PLANS/EXECUTION-STATUS.md:11 fix:trivial conf:high prior:AUDIT-1 S22.1 (recorded fixed by ORCH); regressed by 3a30a91
EVIDENCE: Line 11: "P08 in progress (rounds 0 to 11c accepted and pushed; paused after 11c ...; 12a, 12b, checkpoint (c), the battery and the plan documents left"; line 10 "Last commit | P08 round 11c d27d05b"; line 12 "It is taken again for round 12a". git log shows 75a845f (round 12a) and 3a30a91 (round 12b) committed; the same file's P08 row (361) and Next work (369) say every round through 12b is built. `git show 3a30a91 -- PLANS/EXECUTION-STATUS.md` changed only the hunks at 358 and 366
IMPACT: KICKOFF and CLAUDE.md send each new session to EXECUTION-STATUS first; its Current state tells the next orchestrator to take the lock for round 12a and lists 12a and 12b as open, so a session can re-dispatch two finished rounds (the token waste the owner has forbidden) or record the wrong lock package, while Next work further down says checkpoint (c)
FIX: Update the Last commit, Packages and Lock rows (10-12) to the 12b state: last commit 3a30a91, rounds 0 to 12b built and pushed, left: checkpoint (c), the battery and the plan documents; lock taken again for checkpoint (c)

### FIX-A1-09 [medium] The AUDIT-1 X1.1 policy reset closes a live store silently: nothing alerts the owner and his settings screen still says «الشراء مفتوح»
supabase/migrations/20260930120000_audit_fixes.sql:146 RUNTIME fix:small conf:high prior:AUDIT-1 X1.1 (fixed; this is the fix's consequence now that checkout is live)
EVIDENCE: policies_reset_approval sets `policy_revisions = '{}'` and only `insert into public.audit_events (... 'commerce.policies_reset' ...)` (lines 141-149); it neither turns checkout_enabled off nor calls finance.owner_alert (P08's alert mail, 20261002100000_payment_core.sql:420). From then on checkout_quote reports `'checkoutEnabled', v_settings.checkout_enabled and ... and v_settings.policy_revisions <> '{}'` = false (20261002110000_checkout_payment.sql:351-357), so the cart and checkout say «الشراء غير متاح حاليًا، ويفتح قريبًا.», and checkout_create answers POLICIES_NOT_CONFIGURED (459-460). The owner's screen reads the raw flag: CommerceSettingsForm.tsx:251 `{row.checkoutEnabled ? 'الشراء مفتوح' : 'الشراء مغلق'}`; AdminHome.tsx has no store-state line at all; the publish bar (src/components/admin/PublishBar.tsx) says nothing about it when the document is a policy, and src/admin/collections/policies.ts:5-6 confirms editors draft and publish policy text
IMPACT: Once the store is open (after E02), any publish of a new seq of an approved policy, by an editor (content_assert_publisher allows owner and editor) or by a scheduled publish at night (publish_due), stops every sale until the owner happens to re-approve with a fresh TOTP. He is never told, and the one screen that shows the switch tells him the store is open; Anas loses sales for as long as nobody notices. The rule itself is intended (docs/operations.md:723-731); the missing signal is the defect, and it only matters now that checkout and payments exist
FIX: In policies_reset_approval, after the audit insert, add `perform finance.owner_alert('policies_reset', v_doc || ':' || coalesce(v_seq::text, 'removed'), jsonb_build_object('policy', v_doc))` with a one-line Arabic template in the order-mail renderer; and in CommerceSettingsForm show «الشراء مغلق حتى تُعتمد السياسات» when checkoutEnabled is true and no revision is approved
PROOF: On the local stack with checkout on and policies approved: `select policy_revisions, checkout_enabled from finance.commerce_settings` (non-empty, true); as an editor JWT insert a new content_versions row for ('policies','refund') and `select public.publish_version('policies','refund',<new seq>)`; then `select policy_revisions, checkout_enabled from finance.commerce_settings` -> '{}' and still true; `select count(*) from finance.email_outbox where kind = 'owner_alert' and created_at > now() - interval '1 minute'` -> 0 (wrong: the owner should be told); POST checkout quote -> checkoutEnabled false while /admin/settings shows «الشراء مفتوح»

### FIX-A2-03 [medium] A policy republish silently closes the live store: the owner's screen still says «الشراء مفتوح» and nothing alerts him
src/components/admin/CommerceSettingsForm.tsx:251 fix:small conf:high prior:AUDIT-1 X1.1 (reset by design); AUDIT-2 I48 (2) (readiness folded into the quote, not into the owner's screen); same defect as this audit's wave A DB-CORE-01, found independently from the AUDIT-2 I48 angle: merge
EVIDENCE: AUDIT-2's I48 (2) fix folded readiness into what buyers see: checkout_quote answers `'checkoutEnabled', v_settings.checkout_enabled and v_settings.seller_legal_name is not null and v_settings.seller_registration is not null and v_settings.policy_revisions <> '{}'::jsonb` (supabase/migrations/20261002110000_checkout_payment.sql:352-356). The owner's switch box shows only the stored flag: `<p>{row.checkoutEnabled ? 'الشراء مفتوح' : 'الشراء مغلق'}</p>`. AUDIT-1 X1.1's trigger empties policy_revisions whenever an approved policy gets a new seq (20260930120000_audit_fixes.sql:141-149) and writes only an audit row `commerce.policies_reset`: no finance.owner_alert, nothing in orders_alerts(), nothing on AdminHome (grep: no reader of policies_reset or policy_revisions in src/ besides this form's list line). Editors may publish policies (content_assert_publisher allows owner and editor; content_versions_insert allows owner and editor; docs/operations.md:712 «Editors draft and publish policy text»), and PublishBar offers scheduling for every collection.
IMPACT: Once P08 opens the shop: an editor's typo fix to the delivery policy, or a policy publish scheduled for the night, closes checkout for every buyer («الشراء غير متاح حاليًا، ويفتح قريبًا.» on the cart and checkout) while the owner's settings screen still reads «الشراء مفتوح» and no mail or home line tells him; sales stop until he happens to open the settings and notices «لم تُعتمد بعد». Existing orders are unaffected.
FIX: Show the effective state in the switch box (closed while policyRevisions is empty or the seller is unset, saying why, e.g. «الشراء مغلق: اعتمد السياسات المنشورة»), and in policies_reset_approval queue finance.owner_alert('policies_reset', <doc>, …) when checkout_enabled is true, so the owners are mailed and the home's alert counts show it.

### OPS-PRIVACY-01 [medium] The restore runbook never enables pg_cron/pg_net before the restore, never deploys the Edge Functions, and never repoints the site, the Resend webhook and the browser at the new project
docs/operations.md:1332 fix:small conf:high prior:Related, not the same: EF-ADMIN-01 (wave A) shows a project restored or created after 2025-11-01 has no legacy service_role key, so even deployed functions fail; the restore runbook should also name that check.
EVIDENCE: "A real restore into a new hosted project" has nine steps: extract (1), psql restore (2), Vault (3), cron (4), `supabase secrets set` plus a contact-form smoke test (5), Auth settings (6), Storage upload (7), deletion ledger (8), delete the extract (9). No step runs `supabase functions deploy` (grep of docs/ for 'functions deploy' finds nothing; only PLANS/WORK-PACKAGES.md:139, a future P11 file, names it). No step changes the Pages build's NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL / NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY and rebuilds, although docs/development.md:45-47 says they are "used by the build, the browser admin and the media URLs" (a static export bakes them in). Step 5 says the new <project-ref> changes FUNCTIONS_PUBLIC_URL and the Moyasar webhook address, but not the Resend webhook endpoint `https://<project-ref>.supabase.co/functions/v1/resend-webhook` (operations.md:340). Supabase's backup/restore guide, which step 2 follows (https://supabase.com/docs/guides/platform/migrating-within-supabase/backup-restore, fetched 2026-10-07), lists as gotchas to do before restoring: "If any non-default extensions were used in the old database, enable the Extensions" (pg_cron and pg_net here: 20260925120000_content_versions_and_publishing.sql:9, 20260927090000_static_site_and_functions.sql:23), and says Edge Functions, secrets and environment variables are not migrated. Step 2 names only the supabase_admin/cli_login_postgres caveat; scripts/restore-check.mjs:193-203 handles the extension caveat in the rehearsal, the manual runbook does not. Nor does any step say to stop the old project when it still runs (a restore after a bad data change rather than a lost project): its pg_cron, Vault secrets and function secrets keep sending mail and reconciling with the same Resend and Moyasar keys.
IMPACT: An operator restoring after losing the project ends step 5 with a database, secrets and Vault values but no functions: the smoke test's contact POST and every checkout, payment, outbox, orders, download and admin call answer 404 on the new project, and the public site and admin still call the old (lost) project's URL and key. Bounces and complaints keep going to the dead Resend endpoint, so suppression stops. The runbook gives no next step, so the shop stays down during the incident the runbook exists for.
FIX: Before step 2: enable pg_cron and pg_net in the new project (Database, Extensions). Before the smoke test: supabase link --project-ref <new> then supabase functions deploy (every function; per-function verify_jwt comes from supabase/config.toml); set the Pages project NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL and NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY to the new project and redeploy; edit the Resend webhook endpoint to the new project ref and set RESEND_WEBHOOK_SECRET to its signing secret; check that the functions can reach the database with the new project's keys (wave A EF-ADMIN-01). If the old project is still up, first pause it or delete its Vault jobs_secret and function secrets, so only one project sends mail and talks to Moyasar.

### OPS-PRIVACY-02 [medium] After a restore the deletion ledger is replayed only at step 8, after the mail and job secrets are live, so mail an erasure removed can go out again
docs/operations.md:1405 fix:trivial conf:high prior:none
EVIDENCE: Steps 3-5 re-create Vault `functions_url`/`jobs_secret`, every cron job, and the function secrets including RESEND_API_KEY, then send a real contact message; step 8 ("Reapply the deletion ledger before the site reopens") comes after. privacy_buyer_erase drops a buyer's unsent mail and deletes their notification rows (20261002170000_stats_disputes.sql:777-782); privacy_erase_contacts drops pending notices (20260927140000_privacy_requests.sql:167-169). A restored older backup brings those rows back as `pending`/`confirmed`, and from step 5 `email-outbox` (every minute) and `availability-sweep` (every minute) send them.
IMPACT: A buyer or contact-form visitor who was erased after the backup was taken can receive a receipt, order-link, confirmation or availability mail, or have their contact message re-sent to staff, during the restore window, before the ledger line that erased them is re-run. That is processing of data the owner promised to delete (PDPL), on the path the ledger exists to protect.
FIX: Move the replay of the ledger's `privacy_erase_contacts` and `privacy_buyer_erase` lines to right after step 2 (they are SQL only and need no secret), before any Vault secret, cron schedule or function secret is set; keep the `revoke` and `privacy_erase_staff` lines where the team screen works, and say that no mail is sent until both are done.

### OPS-PRIVACY-03 [medium] No secret-rotation procedure exists, and the only Vault instruction given (vault.create_secret) fails for a secret that already exists
docs/operations.md:373 fix:small conf:high prior:none for the runbook; EF-ADMIN-18 (Resend refusal exhausts mail) and EF-ACCESS-04/EF-ACCESS-08 (pepper) are code-side neighbours
EVIDENCE: `grep -n -i rotat docs/*.md` finds no rotation procedure for any credential. The one Vault instruction is "Hosted setup (P11): `supabase secrets set JOBS_SECRET=…`, then in the SQL editor `select vault.create_secret('<the same value>', 'jobs_secret')`" (operations.md:373-375). Supabase's Vault guide (https://supabase.com/docs/guides/database/vault) says a secret's name is unique and changes go through `vault.update_secret('<uuid>', '<new value>', ...)`. JOBS_SECRET lives in two places that must match: the function secret checked by the `outbox` function and Vault `jobs_secret` sent by `outbox_kick()`/`payments_kick()`/`media_sweep_kick()` (20260927150000_outbox_due_since.sql:67-78, 20261002100000_payment_core.sql:1661-1672, 20260927120000_rebuild_delivery_and_media_sweep.sql:149-157). MOYASAR_WEBHOOK_SECRET must equal the Secret Token typed in Moyasar's dashboard; tokens.ts:3-4 says TOKEN_HASH_PEPPER cannot be rotated without killing every mailed link.
IMPACT: When a key leaks (Moyasar secret key, webhook secret, Resend API key or webhook secret, Turnstile secret, the jobs secret, the Pages deploy hook URL, a Supabase secret key, the analytics token) the operator has no steps: following the setup line for a new jobs secret raises a unique-name error and leaves the function and Vault values out of step, so every pg_cron kick is refused (no mail, no payment reconciliation, no media sweep) until someone notices; changing the Moyasar webhook secret on one side only makes every webhook 401 until Moyasar drops it after its last retry. A leaked deploy hook lets anyone spend the 500 Pages builds a month. Revoking the old Resend key before the new one is set exhausts every queued mail on its first attempt (wave A EF-ADMIN-18), so the order of the steps matters.
FIX: Add a "Rotating a secret" section to operations.md with, per secret: where it is regenerated, the exact `supabase secrets set` and `select vault.update_secret((select id from vault.secrets where name = '<name>'), '<new>')` commands, the order (for JOBS_SECRET and MOYASAR_WEBHOOK_SECRET: both sides within the same minute, then check the next job_runs row or a webhook), what breaks meanwhile, and that TOKEN_HASH_PEPPER is not rotated (a leak means re-issuing links, an owner decision). For a replaced Pages deploy hook (the same path fixes «بناء الموقع: فاشل» after five failed tries): update pages_deploy_hook with vault.update_secret, then run select public.site_build_request(); so the next minute builds without waiting for a publish.

### OPS-PRIVACY-05 [medium] No procedure for a webhook outage or a buyer's double-charge claim, although a second payment on a paid invoice, an unmapped payment and a dashboard refund or void reach the ledger only through the webhook
docs/operations.md:947 RUNTIME fix:small conf:high prior:Neighbours in wave A: EF-MONEY-02 (a refused webhook leaves no trace), EF-MONEY-09 (recheck cannot see a void), P08-CKPT-03/CONTRACT-02/EF-ADMIN-20 (no health signal for the payments job). None of them asks for the operator procedure.
EVIDENCE: A paid attempt gets `next_check_at = null` (20261002100000_payment_core.sql:1164), and `payments_kick()` wakes the job only for rows with `next_check_at <= now()` (payment_core.sql:1653-1660), so nothing re-reads a paid invoice. The runbook says only the webhook settles by payment id (payments-runbook.md:369, item 14) and that external_refund/provider_status come from "a webhook or a check" (payments-runbook.md:254). Moyasar retries a failed delivery 5 times over about 3 h 41 min, then drops it (https://docs.moyasar.com/docs/api/other/webhooks/webhook-reference.md). grep of docs for 'outage', 'twice', 'double' finds no procedure; the reconciliation screen (operations.md:947-964) lists only what the ledger already holds. The uncommitted round M1a migration keeps `next_check_at = null` on a paid attempt (20261007100000_fable_audit_payments.sql:478).
IMPACT: If the payments function is down or misconfigured longer than Moyasar's retries (a bad deploy, a webhook-secret mismatch, a paused project), a buyer's second payment on the same invoice, a payment on an unmapped invoice, or a refund or void made in Moyasar's dashboard is never recorded: no review payment, no owner alert, money held at Moyasar invisible in the admin, and a buyer who writes 'I was charged twice' meets an owner with no steps to find it until the settlement file.
FIX: Add a section to operations.md (after a webhook outage, or when a buyer says he paid twice): how to notice an outage (paid orders with no row in «إشعارات الدفع»; Moyasar dashboard webhook attempts); list Moyasar's payments for the window in its dashboard; for each, find the order by the invoice metadata and press «أعد الفحص» on its paid attempt (it re-reads the invoice and files a second charged payment as a review payment; it cannot see a void, EF-MONEY-09); record a dashboard refund or void with «تسجيل استرداد خارجي», which fetches the payment itself; refund a duplicate from its review payment.
PROOF: With the emulator: pay an order (webhook on), then POST /__emulator/config {"autoWebhook":false} and POST /__emulator/pay {invoiceId, status:'paid', force:true}; wait 5 minutes and run the payments job: expect no finance.payment_reviews row and no owner_alert (the gap). Then call the admin payment-recheck on the paid attempt: expect one payment_reviews row with reason SECOND_PAYMENT (what the procedure relies on).

### OPS-PRIVACY-08 [medium] The privacy data map omits several holdings and every PDPL-specific element E08 will need
docs/privacy-data-map.md:7 OWNER fix:small conf:high prior:none; DB-COMMERCE-07 (customer profile) and DB-OPS-12 (export misses downloads) are separate
EVIDENCE: Not in the table: (a) the media library and the site's photos and films of identifiable people, children included (D44), with private originals kept 'as uploaded' so camera and GPS EXIF stays (docs/media-rights.md:42-46) and a free-text `rights` note (20260926090000_media_library.sql:28); (b) the seller's legal name, address and freelance-certificate number in finance.commerce_settings, snapshotted into every finance.orders.seller, with the name and the registration printed on every receipt (supabase/functions/_shared/email.ts:377-379) (only `approved_by` is listed, line 30); (c) Supabase's API gateway logs record each caller's cf-connecting-ip, x-real-ip and user-agent for every function and Data API call (https://supabase.com/docs/guides/observability/log-field-reference, "Captured HTTP headers": the API Gateway logs capture cf-connecting-ip, cf-ipcountry, user-agent, referer and x-real-ip), while the Providers row says only 'platform logs per plan' and operations.md:117 says 'no raw IP is ever stored or logged'; (d) staff IP addresses and user agents in auth.sessions, auth.audit_log_entries and auth.mfa_challenges (the auth row says 'sign-in history, sessions'). And no row or section gives a lawful basis per purpose, where each processor stores the data (the Supabase project region, Resend, Cloudflare), the deadline for answering a request, or what to do after a breach; grep of docs/ and PLANS/ for 'PDPL', 'region', 'breach' finds nothing. For copies held by providers the map tells the person to go to them ('Moyasar keeps its own payment records and Resend its sent mail, under their own policies: the person asks them', privacy-data-map.md:88), with no word on whether those providers act for Anas and so are his to handle.
IMPACT: E08 (approved privacy text, processor/location review, tested request procedure) cannot be closed from this map, and a privacy policy drafted from it would leave out children's images, the seller's own address, IP logging by the hosting provider and cross-border hosting, which are the points a Saudi PDPL review is likely to ask about.
FIX: Add rows (a)-(d) with purpose, readers and retention, and a short PDPL section listing for E08: the controller and contact, the lawful basis per purpose, each processor and its storage location, the request deadline, the breach procedure, the accounting retention period and how requests reach the providers that act for him. The content and its legal reading are the owner's with E08; this audit names the gaps only.

### OPS-PRIVACY-09 [medium] No runbook for a paused Supabase project, although the site launches on the Free plan and the plan forbids keep-alive traffic
docs/operations.md:18 OWNER fix:small conf:high prior:EF-ADMIN-01 (wave A) is the code consequence of a resume without legacy keys; this is the missing procedure
EVIDENCE: costs.md:7 and :12: the site launches on free plans and 'Free projects are paused after 1 week of inactivity'; DECISIONS.md build policy: 'No paid Images and no fake heartbeats'. grep -n -i 'pause' docs/*.md finds only those two costs.md lines. Supabase: a Free project is inactive when it 'does not receive sufficient user database activity over the past week'; it is resumed with «Resume project» in the dashboard (https://supabase.com/docs/guides/platform/free-project-pausing.md).
IMPACT: In a quiet week before or after launch the project pauses: the static pages still load, but the contact form, availability sign-ups, staff sign-in, scheduled publishing, the outbox and every pg_cron job stop, and the owner home cannot say so because it is down too. Nobody has the steps to notice it, resume it and check what resumed (Vault values, the fifteen cron jobs, a function answering with its key, wave A EF-ADMIN-01), so a visitor's message is refused (or Anas assumes it arrived) until someone happens to open the dashboard.
FIX: Add a 'The project was paused' section: the symptoms, «Resume project» in the dashboard within the restore window, then the checks after a resume (`select jobname from cron.job`, `select name from vault.secrets`, a contact-form smoke test, the owner home's job lines), and the decision the owner takes with E07 (accept pauses until Pro, or move to Pro before launch).

### OPS-PRIVACY-12 [medium] No procedure for removing a staff member in a hurry when that member is an owner, for a compromised owner account, or for an owner who lost his authenticator
docs/privacy-data-map.md:50 OWNER fix:small conf:high prior:EF-ADMIN-02 (revoke keeps sessions) is the code neighbour; the missing procedure is not reported there
EVIDENCE: The only removal steps are 'Revoke them in the team screen' (privacy-data-map.md:52), a privacy procedure; operations.md 'Team invites and admin function errors' covers invites only. Revoke is an owner action at aal2 with a fresh TOTP (staff-admin), and the constraint trigger staff_keep_an_owner refuses any change that leaves no active owner (20260924130000_staff_and_app_server.sql:51-75). Sign-in is a 6-digit email code (supabase/config.toml [auth.email] otp_length = 6), so whoever reads the owner's mailbox can sign in as the owner. Revoke bans but does not end sessions (wave A EF-ADMIN-02). Every step-up action needs a TOTP verified in the last five minutes: refunds, external refunds, disputes, the seller save, policy approval and the checkout switch (operations.md:925-932), and invite, role change and revoke (development.md:111-112); the only TOTP guidance is to enrol one in «الأمان» (operations.md:932), and nothing says what to do when the phone holding it is lost.
IMPACT: If the owner's mailbox or a signed-in device is taken over, or the owner leaves on bad terms, nobody has steps: with a single owner the team screen cannot revoke him at all, and with two the other owner may not know to also change the address, end the sessions and re-enrol TOTP. Meanwhile the intruder reads every buyer's name, address and phone in the orders screens and can publish content (neither needs a TOTP). An owner who loses the phone with his authenticator can no longer refund, revoke a member or close checkout until someone removes the factor outside the admin, and no document says how.
FIX: Add 'Removing access in a hurry' to operations.md: for a non-owner, the team screen; for an owner with a second owner, the same plus changing the account's email in the Supabase dashboard and signing it out everywhere; for the sole owner, the SQL-editor steps (add a trusted owner row first, then revoke, or change the Auth email and delete auth.sessions/auth.mfa_factors for the user) and re-enrolling TOTP; and the decision for the owner to keep a second owner account. Include the lost-authenticator case: remove the factor for that user in the Supabase dashboard (Authentication, the user, MFA) or delete his row in auth.mfa_factors from the SQL editor, then re-enrol in «الأمان».

### OPS-PRIVACY-16 [medium] 'When the Moyasar keys arrive' leaves out the live proof and the hosted prerequisites, so E02 could be declared closed with no live payment ever made
docs/payments-runbook.md:310 OWNER fix:small conf:high prior:EF-MONEY-02 suggests a payment_events check after registering the webhook; the missing live run and hosted prerequisites are not reported
EVIDENCE: The section lists what Anas brings (items 1-7), eight sandbox runs and the open questions; for live it has only item 15 ('a go-live check, not a sandbox run') and 'Before live' (payments-runbook.md:310-317): settle sandbox orders, E03 first, remove the access code, 'set the live secrets and register the live webhook'. No step makes one real payment in live mode after the switch, confirms a finance.payment_events row with live = true arrives (the live webhook is a separate registration whose Secret Token can be mistyped, wave A EF-MONEY-02), and refunds it. DECISIONS.md E02 names 'real sandbox acceptance/live payments' as closure evidence. The hosted sandbox runs also need checkout open, which `commerce_checkout_set` refuses until the seller's legal name and registration are set and policies approved (20261002110000_checkout_payment.sql:736-740, 'أكمل بيانات البائع واعتمد السياسات أولًا.'), plus a published catalog on the hosted project (D37: demo rows reach it only if the owner decides); none of that is in 'What Anas brings' or the run list.
IMPACT: The first live order is the first test of the live key, the live webhook and the live callback: a mistyped live Secret Token or a wrong live base silently loses dashboard refunds and voids (only the job and the return page settle), and a run on the hosted sandbox stops at NOT_READY with no instruction, inviting someone to approve placeholder policies on the real project.
FIX: Add to 'Before live' a final supervised step: switch to live, buy the cheapest item with Anas's own card, check the receipt, a payment_events row with live = true and the order view, refund it in full and see the payment_refunded event, then open checkout; record it as E02's live evidence. Add to 'What Anas brings' (or a prerequisites line for hosted runs) the seller's name and certificate, the policies to approve for the sandbox, and the catalog the runs buy from.

### OPS-PRIVACY-19 [medium] After a restore, orders paid since the backup are gone and the runbook has no step to find them, although Moyasar and Resend still hold what is needed
docs/operations.md:1413 OWNER fix:small conf:high prior:none
EVIDENCE: D35's accepted risk is 'Anything entered after the last backup is lost if the project is lost' (operations.md:1415-1417), written 2026-09-27, before P08. The restore steps (operations.md:1334-1411) end with secrets, Auth, Storage and the ledger; none compares Moyasar with the restored ledger. An order created after the backup has no row, so its invoice maps to no attempt (a later webhook would file UNMAPPED_INVOICE at best), and the buyer's name, address and phone exist nowhere on our side: Moyasar is never sent them (privacy-data-map.md:34), while every invoice carries the order number in its description and metadata (payments-runbook.md:168) and the buyer's receipt «إيصال طلبك رقم …» sits in Resend's sent mail for 30 days (https://resend.com/pricing, Free: 30-day data retention).
IMPACT: If the project is lost a few days after the owner's last `pnpm backup`, every sale made since is invisible after the restore: paid buyers get no file, no shipment and a dead order link, the stock is wrong, and the money is noticed only in the settlement file, by which time Resend's 30 days may have passed and the buyers cannot be reached to refund or deliver.
FIX: Add a restore step before reopening: list Moyasar's payments created since the backup's manifest time, match each to an order number from its invoice metadata, and for those missing from the restored database recover the buyer from Resend's sent receipts (within 30 days) and decide refund or manual fulfilment; and state in D35's accepted risks that sales after the last backup are part of what is lost, which the owner revisits with E07 (Pro's daily backups) before live orders.

### PLAN-GAPS-01 [medium] P09's file list cannot carry a paid booking: the order and payment core it must change are variant-only and absent from the list
PLANS/WORK-PACKAGES.md:115 fix:medium conf:high prior:none; AUDIT-1 and AUDIT-2 did not review P09's plan text against P08's code
EVIDENCE: P09 lists only `src/admin/tables/{services,availability-rules,availability-exceptions}.ts`, `supabase/functions/_shared/{booking,calendar,checkout,email}.ts`, BookingForm, the `bookings`/`calendar` functions, 'finance migrations' and three tests. The schema P07/P08 built is variant-only: `finance.order_items` has `variant_id uuid not null references public.product_variants (id)` (supabase/migrations/20260927160000_catalog_and_checkout.sql:389), and the one commit path `finance.order_try_commit` (20261002100000_payment_core.sql:602-640) locks `public.product_variants ... where v.id in (select i.variant_id ...)` and the reservations only. DATA-AND-SECURITY.md:35 still promises 'Item variant or service exactly one', which no table implements.
IMPACT: A paid consultation (Anas's Q35: one 60-minute session at 500 SAR) must be held, paid, late-paid, refunded and cancelled through `checkout_create`, `apply_verified_payment`/`order_try_commit`, `order_resolve`, the refund functions, `order_email_data`, `orders_list`/`order_detail`, `privacy_buyer_export`, `buyer_retention_purge` and the statistics. None is in P09's list, so the lock allowlist built from it either blocks the work or invites a booking path built beside the verified-payment machinery, where the plan's own 'late paid conflict never bumps another booking' and 'refund/rebook' proofs would break.
FIX: After P08's acceptance and before P09 starts, write a P09 contract like PLANS/P08-CONTRACT.md: the order-line change (variant_id nullable plus a booking/service reference with a one-of check), the slot step inside `order_try_commit` and `order_resolve`, refund-to-cancel semantics, the new mail kinds and their outbox tier, `supabase/config.toml` entries for the new functions, `public/_headers` and `scripts/check-export.mjs` for the new pages; then enumerate those paths in WORK-PACKAGES P09.

### PLAN-GAPS-02 [medium] D44 records the five children's films as consented, but the consent Anas said he would send is not in hand and no plan item tracks it
PLANS/DECISIONS.md:51 OWNER fix:trivial conf:high prior:AUDIT-1 G4.2 (films shipped before consent) was fixed, then D44 re-admitted them on the owner's word; this is the newer record against it
EVIDENCE: D44 (3), 2026-10-01: 'The five films that show children are consented, on Anas's word: they are back in «بدأتُ من هنا» ... and the export check no longer refuses them.' The films ship in content/initial-content.json:250-266 (`46-kid-picnic-jam` to `50-kid-cafe-croissant-jam`). Anas's questionnaire answer Q11 (saved 2026-09-29; sorted 2026-10-02 in the git-ignored BOOK_ASSETS/SORTED_2026-10-02/ANSWERS.md:84) ticks «عندي الموافقة، وسأرسلها للمطوّر مباشرة» but writes «بتأكد من العائله إذا سمحت لي»; the sorter's note: 'The written consent is not in hand ... still owed before launch (E05).' ISSUES I33 only asks how to host the files, E05 does not name it, and NEXT.md's proposed I33 update was never written into PLANS.
IMPACT: At P11 the launcher follows D44 and I33 and publishes five films of identifiable children on the public site while the family's permission, by Anas's own note, was still to be asked and the promised consent never arrived. If the family declines, the films come down after publication; images of minors are personal data for which a guardian's consent is the expected basis under PDPL (owner must confirm).
FIX: Record in ISSUES (I33 or E05) that the consent Anas promised for films 46-50 is owed and is a launch blocker for those five films; keep them out of the hosted content bootstrap (I41) until the owner files it. Reconciling D44 with Q11 is the owner's call, not an agent's.

### PLAN-GAPS-03 [medium] Gate E02 still reads 'freelance certificate ready', but the certificate's speciality is graphic design and the shop will sell a book and family food products
PLANS/DECISIONS.md:75 OWNER fix:trivial conf:high prior:HANDOFF.md:9 mentions the question without a gate or owner; not in AUDIT-1 or AUDIT-2
EVIDENCE: E02 row: 'Anas: Moyasar eligibility (freelance certificate ready), test/live account, enabled methods'. Anas's answers sorted 2026-10-02 (git-ignored ANSWERS.md:85, NEXT.md section 1): the certificate issued 2026-09-24, valid to 2027-09-24, 'Its speciality is «تصميم الجرافيك» (Graphic Design)'; the shop is to sell the book and the family's jams, ghee and pastes (Q10). Moyasar FAQ (https://moyasar.com/en/resources/faqs/, read 2026-10-07): 'Does the CR activity have to match the online store activity? No, but it should be related or compatible. All activities must be allowed by SAMA regulations.' and before live 'we need to review your store basics like products, images, pricing, policies, and communication channels.' HANDOFF.md:9 parks 'certificate speciality and Moyasar's acceptance' as 'not P08's'; no PLANS file assigns it.
